│                  if (tlen &gt; 0) {                                          │
│                      tok[tlen] = &#x27;\0&#x27;;                             │
│                      tlen = 0;                                               │
│                                                                              │
│                      if (state == ST_WAIT_P3) {                              │
│                          if (strcmp(tok, &quot;P3&quot;) == 0) {             │
│                              write(out, &quot;P3\n&quot;, 3);                │
│                              state = ST_HDR;                                 │
│                              hidx = 0;                                       │
│                          }                                                   │
│                      } else if (state == ST_HDR) {                           │
│                          header[hidx++] = atoi(tok);                         │
│                          if (hidx == 3) {                                    │
│                              char hdr[32];                                   │
│                              int len = snprintf(hdr, sizeof(hdr), &quot;%d   │
│  %d\n%d\n&quot;, header[0], header[1], header[2]);                           │
│                              write(out, hdr, len);                           │
│                              total = (long long)header[0] * header[1] * 3;   │
│                              state = ST_DATA;                                │
│                              produced = 0;                                   │
│                          }                                                   │
│                      } else { // ST_DATA                                     │
│                          int isnum = 1;                                      │
│                          for (size_t i = 0; tok[i]; ++i) {                   │
│                              if (!isdigit((unsigned char)tok[i])) { isnum =  │
│  0; break; }                                                                 │
│                          }                                                   │
│                          if (isnum &amp;&amp; produced &lt; total) {         │
│                              // emit this number                             │
│                              write(out, tok, strlen(tok));                   │
│                              write(out, &quot; &quot;, 1);                   │
│                              produced++;                                     │
│                              if (produced % 3 == 0) write(out,               │
│  &quot;\n&quot;, 1);                                                         │
│                          }                                                   │
│                      }                                                       │
│                  }                                                           │
│              } else {                                                        │
│                  if (tlen &lt; (int)sizeof(tok) - 1) tok[tlen++] = ch;       │
│              }                                                               │
│          }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│          // Final flush (if last token wasn&#x27;t followed by whitespace)   │
│          if (tlen &gt; 0) {                                                  │
│              tok[tlen] = &#x27;\0&#x27;;                                     │
│              if (state == ST_WAIT_P3) {                                      │
│                  if (strcmp(tok, &quot;P3&quot;) == 0) {                     │
│                      write(out, &quot;P3\n&quot;, 3);                        │
│                      state = ST_HDR; hidx = 0;                               │
│                  }                                                           │
│              } else if (state == ST_HDR) {                                   │
│                  header[hidx++] = atoi(tok);                                 │
│                  if (hidx == 3) {                                            │
│                      char hdr[32];                                           │
│                      int len = snprintf(hdr, sizeof(hdr), &quot;%d           │
│  %d\n%d\n&quot;, header[0], header[1], header[2]);                           │
│                      write(out, hdr, len);                                   │
│                      total = (long long)header[0] * header[1] * 3;           │
│                      state = ST_DATA;                                        │
│                      produced = 0;                                           │
│                  }                                                           │
│              } else if (state == ST_DATA) {                                  │
│                  int isnum = 1;                                              │
│                  for (size_t i = 0; tok[i]; ++i) {                           │
│                      if (!isdigit((unsigned char)tok[i])) { isnum = 0;       │
│  break; }                                                                    │
│                  }                                                           │
│                  if (isnum &amp;&amp; produced &lt; total) {                 │
│                      write(out, tok, strlen(tok)); write(out, &quot;         │
│  &quot;, 1);                                                                 │
│                      produced++;                                             │
│                      if (produced % 3 == 0) write(out, &quot;\n&quot;, 1);   │
│                  }                                                           │
│              }                                                               │
│          }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│          close(out);                                                         │
│          close(p[0]);                                                        │
│          int status = 0;                                                     │
│          waitpid(pid, &amp;status, 0);                                       │
│          return (WIFEXITED(status) ? WEXITSTATUS(status) : 1);               │
│      }                                                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
